JVM: media 141/141, visual+phone+options 149 (2 pre-existing skips). Installed/physical unverified.
- Real providers (opt-in `RealProviderLookupTest`, run locally 2026-11-05): TVmaze, MusicBrainz/CAA and Open Library each returned an AUTOMATIC match with a decoded image through the production controller; no release tags, extension or hash were sent; offline restart restored everything with zero requests. Skipped without `CHUNKS_REAL_MEDIA_LOOKUP=1` (2/2 skipped, verified with `--rerun`).
- **Root cause of the missing notification prompt (run 42098623009, head `b2d1445`):** the new system-window diagnostic read `Pixel Launcher isn't responding, Close app, Wait`. An emulator launcher ANR covered the prompt; Chunks was responsive. The HTTPS journey now waits up to 40 s, chooses **Wait** on another app's ANR (never Close), logs it as `foreign-anr-waited`, and still fails if the ANR names Chunks.
- **Root cause of the rTorrent-stage intermittent (run 42100816764, head `d469a59`):** the safe receipt's first frame is `RTorrentRemote.kt:1589`, the request write, right after `metadata-already-resolved`, where the test's own read-only `readActivity` runs. Every request nginx logged returned 200, so the connection dropped in the emulator's `adb reverse` tunnel before reaching the proxy. The journey's direct read-only rTorrent checks now retry `IOException` up to 4 times, 1 s apart, and log `CHUNKS_READ_RETRY`. Its two cleanup mutations are never retried. The same run's HTTPS phone part passed (`verified=true`).
- **Second, separate mechanism (run 42102743623, head `32cb42e`):** the disposable rTorrent (Ubuntu's apt libtorrent `.so.21`) aborted with `Caught internal_error: TrackerController cannot set timeout when inactive` in `TrackerController::do_timeout`. This came right after a started `.torrent`-link Add whose announce (`127.0.0.1:1`) refuses connections. nginx then answered 502 (SCGI connection refused) and the bounded read retry rightly gave up. The previous run's rTorrent log has no crash, so that one was the tunnel. This is an upstream server-side race, not a Chunks fault. The failure-only diagnostic step now raises `Disposable rTorrent crashed` with the first line. Fixture or server-version changes to avoid it are not made here, because the P0 fixtures stay as qualified.
- Native Add verify failed at `32cb42e` with both shards 0 failures: `FooterImeInstalledInstrumentedTest.footerYieldsToTheSoftKeyboardAndReturnsAfterSearchKey` skipped because the emulator showed no soft keyboard (it passed at `d469a59` with the same field). That made 35 counted skips against the ceiling of 34. It was identified by matching its hashed case ID. It repeated at `184fa07`, so `native_add_prebuilt.sh` now sets `show_ime_with_hard_keyboard 1` before instrumentation: an emulator reporting a hardware keyboard hides the IME.
- **Third, separate mechanism (run 42106810889 attempt 1, head `7479b46`):** first frame `MainActivityRealMagnetJourney.kt:396`, the 15 s UI wait for "Downloading payload from peers.". The controlled peer had uploaded only about 576 KB of `magnet2`, so no fresh download rate was observed and the row truthfully read Started. All requests were 200 and the server didn't crash. That is a test-rig peer stall, not a product fault. The rerun passed. Open: understand why the local controlled peer stalls.
- The API29 Android 10 dialog journey failed once at `d1f95f3` with a Compose `Detected multithreaded access to SnapshotStateObserver` in `dispatchDraw` (no Chunks frame); it passed on the next head `b2d1445` with no related change. Recorded as an intermittent; not explained.
- Run 42080666983 (head `18d6085`) failed at `added-stopped` with the known missing notification prompt (`foreground=android`), before the system-window text diagnostic landed; nothing new.

## Integrated HTTPS Add and one-time phone destinations (#1301)

**Source:** immutable qualification tag `ux-qualification-f07a8331f81f`, checkout `f07a8331f81f204d4c44abb8339a3185f314051a`, PR head `d54201beb7d362ff14138c730834489290be5748`. Merge: `19ff314521bac6d06e45e9a3a3267a30e02c5383`. This includes the already merged media package #1302; no media feature or font work was added by this campaign.

**Observed journeys:** installed production HTTPS retrieval, private-address refusal/recovery, non-mutating source review, cancelled-review recovery, explicit Add stopped, exact one-time App Downloads root/subfolder, Combined round trip and recreation, actual Start, received and native-verified publisher pieces, Stop/cache checkpoint, independent exact piece readback, next Add using the unchanged saved Public Downloads default, and keep-data removal. Real rTorrent also fetched genuine publisher HTTPS URLs through the installed production Add screen, verified exact hash/Place, received pieces, used UI Stop/Start with further bytes, and removed exact jobs while preserving unrelated/precompleted inventory. These are bounded piece outcomes; no complete ISO/movie download is claimed.

**Production changes:** current-transfer Place is separate from explicit Save phone default; fresh verified choices resolve the exact transient root, and unavailable/replaced choices block rather than silently redirect. Non-secret root/subfolder/Start intent survives navigation/recreation; source URLs remain memory-only. Success or explicit review Cancel clears the current destination. No relocation. The actual rTorrent journey exposed `.torrent` Add's missing live progress observation; both source types now use the existing exact-hash/Place read-only poller. Seven Android 15 MediaProvider FUSE aborts with the default memory-mapped backend led to upstream libtorrent4j 2.1.0-39's documented Android compatibility option, `SessionParams.setPosixDiskIO()`, for the production phone holder. Its portable backend is single-threaded; physical peak throughput is unmeasured. R8 exercises this shipping configuration. Notification-timing and resume-probe experiments were discarded.

| Native layout atlas [41973481249](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41973481249) | 3 | 0 | Passed; large-text PNG inspected, not human acceptance |
| Combined two real servers [41973481231](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41973481231) | 2 phases | 0 | Passed, including process restoration |
| Installed remote delete-data [41973481707](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41973481707) | 1 | 0 | Passed; actual upstream PHP collector and canary |
| Legacy/modern real rTorrent [41973481426](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41973481426) | Not aggregated | Not aggregated | Both jobs passed; individual native-start and interop gates reject skips; no invented whole-run count |
| Documentation [41973481568](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41973481568) | Offline checks | N/A | Passed |

**Efficiency and scope:** phone HTTPS and real-rTorrent HTTPS reuse one installed APK pair, emulator and public-fixture server. Native Add builds once for both shards. API29 batches permission, destination, persistence, resume, service, intake/process-death and revocation/recovery on one installation/session. The separate magnet publication gate starts with fresh server payloads to avoid accepting pre-existing bytes; its offered APK is pulled back and byte-compared with the installed package. Independent suites run concurrently on separate disposable fixtures. No protected host writes. Every opt-in native skip remains separately identifiable; only the exact new HTTPS case has an additional dedicated-fixture skip allowance, and arbitrary extra skips still fail.

**Development receipts, not final acceptance:** focused destination JVM 20/0 (320×700 and 412×892 at 200%, persistence, semantics, 48dp and rendered AA contrast); remote component checks 15 executed/1 explicitly opt-in real-fetch skip; relevant offline contracts 62/0 plus API29 harness negatives. A broad offline discovery attempt ran 386 with four unchanged font-shaping subtest failures and five missing optional shapely/cv2 imports; it is not a green whole-repository receipt and the font lane was untouched. Seven failed native-abort diagnostics: `41962055344`, `41962874921`, `41963797727`, `41964873985`, `41965885700`, `41967527742`, `41969248191`. POSIX run [41970494483](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41970494483) passed its actual phone test but correctly failed the strict parser on custom diagnostic statuses; diagnostics moved to Logcat, leaving the parser strict. [41972427108](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41972427108) then passed phone 1/0, with an empty crash buffer; remote failed at `MainActivityRealMagnetJourney.kt:378` after real verified pieces, exposing the missing metainfo poller. Early superseded foundation/API29/native jobs and seven accidentally duplicated PR jobs were cancelled; they are not accepted evidence. Previously green superseded R8/atlas/Combined/delete runs are historical and were requalified for the assembled source.

**Artifact:** [Download the exact installed Preview and receipts](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41974228875/artifacts/13390879645), retained 14 days. `chunks-magnet-verified-preview-f07a8331f81f.apk`, SHA-256 `0606a26d221b039ea0b5ed706c4451f02d319cbdcf76337caaed9858dbf0b414`; paired `CHUNKS-BUILD-SHA256.txt`, `RECEIPT.json`, JUnit and independent peer receipt. The separately built HTTPS APK has a different digest; it is not substituted for the magnet-qualified offered bytes. Debug signed, separately installable Preview; no publisher signing, physical/OEM or human acceptance. Remaining owner tiers: Pixel 6 Pro ordinary daily-use and background/storage behaviour, human TalkBack/focus/visual/keyboard/clipboard notice, removable SD/vendor SAF/provider and third-party IME specificity. Authenticated external tracker/provider accounts require separately authorized access. Existing disabled unsafe ZIP/library operations #809 remain disabled. No count of partial trace rows is treated as a missing-feature backlog.

## 2026-11-04 Connect-transition instability in installed Combined (diagnosis and fix)

Three installed Combined failures all occurred in the Connect → Transfers transition while the soft keyboard was still closing:

| Run | Attempt | Failure |
| --- | --- | --- |
| #1278 Combined [41884156514](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41884156514) | 1 | `CalledFromWrongThreadException` in focus invalidation |
| #1283 Combined [41901115836](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41901115836) | 1 | footer `Options` not composed |
| #1283 Combined [41901115836](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41901115836) | 2 | `performMeasureAndLayout called during measure layout` |

The contributing change is #1274's footer, which yields to the IME. **Fix (this PR):** submitting the connection form (button or Go) hides the keyboard and clears focus before Connect starts, so the transition no longer overlaps an IME animation. The installed Combined journeys also wait for the footer before tapping it. Qualification is the exact-head installed Combined run on this PR. I did not merge #1283's earlier head on reruns.

**Independent review follow-up (not yet merged):** the review found that wrapped Options destinations could overflow a 320dp/200% screen, that the hidden-new-Add notice could reappear for an old Add, that the outcome live region was not merged with its text, that batch selection sat far from its outcomes, and that the spoken rTorrent Add target was not exact. The follow-up commits add a height-bounded, vertically scrolling Options panel (with a 320dp/200% server render variant), a view snapshot for the notice, a merged live region, Batch organize above the list, the exact server label in the spoken Add target, and Privacy & authentication hosted in Options rather than pinned (Lock now stays on top while a policy is active). They also wrap the snapshot filters and Options destinations instead of scrolling sideways, and add clear controls to the snapshot and Organize searches. **These follow-up commits require a fresh exact-head CI run before merge**; the first-head receipts above do not cover them.

**Follow-up head `79c9d2a3` (genuine red, then fixed):** docs [41761160566](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41761160566), foundation [41761160636](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41761160636), R8 [41761160491](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41761160491), atlas [41761160630](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41761160630) and API29 [41761160499](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41761160499) passed. Native Add [41761160521](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41761160521) **failed** on shard 0: 159 executed, 1 failed, `ChunksMainActivityAtlasTest.installedAppNavigatesPopulatedReadOnlyRpcScreens` at line 392, filling rTorrent Add's custom folder. Shard 1 passed. Cause: that head showed the destinations panel with Privacy controls on every non-Transfers route, capped at 45% of the screen. On Add, with the keyboard up, this squeezed the Add list. A new JVM reproduction (`serverAddKeepsItsFieldsEditableBelowDestinations`) shows the Add list at 325dp of a 700dp screen at 320dp/200% on that head. The fix limits non-Transfers routes to a two-row destination strip and keeps Privacy controls in Transfers Options. This is the same test that was historically red for Waves 3–4; this occurrence was caused by this branch, and the red run stays on record.

**Local container evidence (not CI):** JDK 21 with a UTF-8 locale ran the full JVM suite: 1,532 tests, with 2 failures that were JDK-21-only Security Manager tests (`CreateSeedCopyAcquisitionRaceTest`; CI uses JDK 17). Without a UTF-8 locale, `LocalCheckedPayloadReservationsTest` fails on "Café"; that is an environment artifact. In a negative control, the strengthened server render test failed 2/2 against the previous sideways Options row. No physical Pixel 6 Pro, TalkBack-human or protected-host evidence.

## 2026-11-03 Robolectric runtime fetch failure: bounded CI triage evidence

**Observed before any tool change:** [#1194 foundation run 41670063830](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41670063830) built Android sources and ran **1,478** JVM tests with **one failed** (`AuthenticatedTransfersFooterTest`, 36 skipped). The failing test stack named `MavenArtifactFetcher.java:130` and nested `java.net.SocketException`; this is a Robolectric runtime SDK JAR network/download symptom rather than demonstrated incorrect footer behavior. Its exact HTTP/TLS/root cause remains undetermined; the red run cannot serve as positive Compose acceptance. Official Robolectric documentation independently confirms its separate runtime fetch path. The PR owner was informed in an [independent GitHub comment](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/1194) rather than being directed to change valid UX code or repeat unneeded entire campaigns.

**Receipt improvement:** `tools/ci_failure_digest.py` emits a static, advisory `possible-robolectric-runtime-dependency-network-fetch` hint only when both verified markers occur inside a failing `android-jvm` JUnit case. The original test-failure status, executed count, installed Android engine evidence and exact source are untouched. Neither raw test exception, dynamic URL/credentials nor public private-tracker information is emitted. Offline negative controls cover failed-class hints, missing/mixed markers, malformed reports, wrong suite and no false pass. No SDK prefetch or cache change is qualified; measured speed savings are unknown. Canonical decision and limits: [CI-POLICY](CI-POLICY.md).

**Offline evidence:** [Documentation validation 41712119969](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41712119969) passed nine bounded offline controls for network reset, wrong checksum, unexpected POM coordinates, invalid JAR, tampered cached files, unsupported SDK/version, unsafe transport/size and unchanged Gradle test selection. **Real foundation evidence:** [Actions run 41712120036](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41712120036) has **two actual attempts**, both counted in runner cost:

## 2026-11-03 exact search-ceiling and selection-provenance correction

GitHub's [Actions workflow-runs REST documentation](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/actions/workflow-runs) caps filtered result retrieval at 1,000. An exact 1,000-result page series may represent a search boundary rather than a complete historical match set. Following merged pagination [#1193](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/1193), the read-only reporter now refuses **exactly** 1,000 workflow-run matches as well as larger counts and requests narrower dated windows instead. No accepted Android evidence, run/job timestamp, or historical cost total is altered.

The report's machine-readable and human summary now preserve the precise requested live `repository`/`branch`/`created_range` and all-attempt job filter; offline JSON uses a distinct `offline-snapshot` mode without inventing a branch. Deterministic negative tests cover the 1,000 boundary and both JSON selection modes. This is reproducibility and false-completeness prevention, not a measured runner-minute or billable-minute saving.


## 2026-11-03 bounded, complete Actions cost-report pagination

The existing read-only `tools/ci_runner_cost_report.py` previously rejected any branch with over 100 runs even though its suggested date-scope workaround did not exist. Live `GET /actions/runs?branch=ux/73-wave2-phone-first-and-footer-20261103&created=2026-11-03..2026-11-03&per_page=100` returned **54** records, confirming the documented GitHub date filter. The earlier main-push study already needed **three pages / 202 records** (October 27–November 2). Neither observation triggered a build, and no runner-minute savings can be attributed to changing a read-only report.

The additive reporter change supports `--created YYYY-MM-DD..YYYY-MM-DD`, complete numbered workflow-run pages, and full `filter=all` job pages (preserving old-attempt cost). It checks page counts, duplicate IDs, invalid dates, live unsettled runs and GitHub's filtered 1,000-run search limit. Offline tests specifically exercise 101-run/101-job samples and intentionally malformed pages. Missing or moving pages are errors, not a valid partial measurement. Date-scoped results must not be presented as whole-branch lifetime totals. The change touches no app/Android/BitTorrent/rTorrent execution or CI workflow scheduling. See [CI-POLICY](CI-POLICY.md) for the live read-only evidence, limitations and invocation.


## 2026-11-03 active CI occupancy and draft supersession evidence

The matching offline controls are executed in the existing documentation validation job; the tool is **advisory** for agents preparing a rapid push, not a mandate to defer legitimate bug fixes. ## 2026-11-03 real-rTorrent Android failure artifact and diagnostic correction

**Original evidence:** [run 41603577160 attempt 1](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41603577160) job `125807846843` failed after 4.667 runner-minutes despite a successful exit from the outer Android-emulator step. The **3,186-byte sanitized failure artifact** (PEER-RECEIPT, RECEIPT and JUnit) explicitly records one `AssertionError`, one instrumented Android test failed, zero passes, app phase `connect-click-attempt` without `connect-submitted`, and whitelisted frames `MainActivityRealMagnetJourney.kt:53/:169`, `SeedhostAndroidAddUiInstrumentedTest.kt:143`. Negative peer and phase gates correctly refused APK acceptance. The same workflow's second job `125810907598` passed on rerun and must not retroactively certify attempt 1. This was a real test failure; the receipt only exposed it with a generic message.

**Correction without false green:** Diagnostic-only `safe_failure_diagnostic` in `tools/finish_installed_magnet_evidence.py` writes and emits a bounded classification, last known whitelisted UI stage and first whitelisted source frame **only after the prior receipt is red**. All real peer, installed test and APK gates are unchanged. New offline tests reject raw URLs, malformed frames and raw test data in surfaced annotations. **Qualified and merged:** [PR #1184](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/1184) was squash-merged at `32dfc4d7f85ab338f7d1fb3e15733e084acfb36b` after exact head `bea475676a001533b903fbc4007258874c9e113b` passed [documentation/offline tests 41619764203](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41619764203) and [genuine installed API35 disposable real-rTorrent acceptance 41619764005](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41619764005). The diagnostic improvement is source- and emulator-qualified, but it does not establish that the independently owned Compose Connect test in [UX #1175](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/1175) has been fixed, nor prove an owner handset or protected host.

## 2026-11-02 P0 magnet causal checkpoint

**Native lifecycle cause independently reproduced:** disposable rTorrent 0.9.8/libtorrent 2.0.8, same generated 2 MiB hash `d69bbdb0e7033fc600af404b395c31ac6c379e02`, same dead tracker and exact destination: `load.normal` + `d.start` showed placeholder state=1/active=1, but after genuine BEP9 became payload state=0/active=0 with zero payload bytes. `load.start` kept the resolved payload state=1/active=1 and received/verified all 2097152 bytes, independently byte-compared. Both exact sessions removed, inventory restored. This is a local real-engine comparison (elapsed runtime was not instrumented), not Android/public-discovery proof. Upstream [0.9.8 DownloadFactory](https://github.com/rakshasa/rtorrent/blob/v0.9.8/src/core/download_factory.cc#L226) persists the load-time `start` flag in `rtorrent_meta_download`; [DownloadList](https://github.com/rakshasa/rtorrent/blob/v0.9.8/src/core/download_list.cc#L693) erases the placeholder and recreates from that stored intent. This explains why native start-on-load matters, independently of dead public swarms. The production correction is already merged #973; #1025 completes honest metadata/payload observation and uncertain-ACK recovery.

**Ordinary public discovery now proven on both versions:** [41390471914](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41390471914), tested checkout `91eada2397b2ae41a4df0c1bb71470dbaa5976e5`, gave the same original Debian amd64 and Sintel magnets to rTorrent and libtorrent, with no webseeds, supplied peers or prepared payload. rTorrent 0.9.8 received 2234245/344166 and verified 1310720/131072 bytes; rTorrent 0.16.26 received 1368472/589438 and verified 262144/262144 bytes. Both metadata sets resolved, real peers observed, exact cleanup/inventory restored, all four actual JUnit outcomes passed. Probe windows were 12.0 and 9.9 seconds. The modern predecessor was inactive because its runner-only destination was not mounted into the container; the corrected valid Place succeeds with the original directory-command load shape. Earlier setup failure also used a removed modern listen-port command. Neither failure establishes a Chunks argument-count defect. [#1027](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/1027) retains this bounded comparison and fail-closed evidence.

**Final-candidate disposable app gate:** [41389460836](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41389460836), checkout `3264158373ed2a0f4fdcb9d11d747b88d650b448` / PR head `7a5591d21bedf69b3752d5e5d6e0fcd03360ad1b`, passed the actual two-source MainActivity lifecycle and negative again in 9.5 minutes, independent source pieces and uploader counts agreed. ## 2026-10-31 native resume metadata and Remove nonclobber review

**#568 remains release G0 RED.** Separate from libtorrent's native payload overwrite (3/4 planted files overwritten in actual [production witness 41130188712](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41130188712)), the consolidated [#812](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/812) admission candidate has three metadata-path integrity surfaces:

1. **`.admission` retirement:** reviewed pre-fix head `c7d902e26c5edbbd21489b9b1a17dd4f2fd78872` called `Files.deleteIfExists(<hash>.admission)` during Remove. Same-UID final path replacement can unlink a foreign directory entry. On candidate `865a82ce7c744eb531b032e4c01e41c2e57fd56f` it is replaced with a non-deleting immutable tombstone; direct regular/symlink/hardlink and actual `removeKeepingData()` foreign-entry preservation tests are committed. **Exact-head documentation validation SUCCESS; JVM, R8, API29/35, lifecycle and disposable protocol were still running at review checkpoint.** Do not count the previous seven green workflows for the modified head.
2. **`.resume` Remove:** `LocalTorrentEngine.removeKeepingData()` still uses `saved.delete()` after JNI session removal. Another actor could substitute an unowned hardlink, regular file or symlink and have its final directory entry unlinked. Omitting the deletion without durable state permits resurrection on process restart because `restore()` enumerates `*.resume`. No accepted fix/test yet.
3. **`.resume.tmp` rewrite:** `writeResume()` still does `FileOutputStream("$hash.resume.tmp")` (truncating any path occupant), then `Files.move(tmp, "$hash.resume", REPLACE_EXISTING, ATOMIC_MOVE)`. This is not a no-replace, owner-pinned snapshot commit; both temp and target can be foreign. No accepted fix/test yet.

**Next executable acceptance:** immutable exclusive resume generation files, original-FD full write/force and checked directory durability, read-only exact-hash restore with ambiguous generation refusal, generation-bound removal intent/commit which cannot resurrect jobs, explicit later same-hash re-Add, and denial on any foreign temp/final directory entry without modifying its bytes *or name*. Fault-inject before/after each persistence boundary, test real native API29/35 and host process death. Existing metadata guards still do **not** prove safe production BitTorrent payload writing. No generic user files, protected hosts or physical-device results were used for this source review.

Product effect: ZIP **inspection** has no named staging-file write/reopen, no filename/mtime cleanup deletion, and close-only anonymous inode lifetime; hostile/unavailable O_TMPFILE/memfd environments refuse rather than downgrade. ## 2026-10-30 G0 RED: persisted checked-Add restore loses foreign-file protection

**New independent native proof:** [PR #733](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/733), candidate head `8e4205b3ba607f3624372fc49d829dd1a2890861`, [real native loopback JVM/JNI run 41121891456](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/41121891456). The **deliberately green diagnostic** executes production `addStoppedChecked` with app-style persisted resume state, closes engine, plants foreign data at the previously empty destination, restores a stopped native handle and submits first Start. The native workflow's four diagnostic cases and five-line `CHUNKS_RESTORE_COLLISION` witness executed successfully, but that green characterization is **negative product evidence**:

- `observed_stopped=true resumed_outcome=VERIFIED`
- `before_sha256=a81511ac006812fe5008019b1b8173107861f600226cf5fcfbf5da6dd81201e2`, `after_restore_sha256=a81511ac006812fe5008019b1b8173107861f600226cf5fcfbf5da6dd81201e2` (foreign file unchanged at restore).
- `expected_sha256=29ee35e1f27d929ad825d65ce49375ada3e0df61b476e5bfbc54a74303cbbfb1`, `after_sha256=29ee35e1f27d929ad825d65ce49375ada3e0df61b476e5bfbc54a74303cbbfb1` (native Start replaced foreign bytes with torrent payload).
- `restore_changed_foreign=false foreign_file_replaced=true expected_torrent_data_present=true`.

**Implication:** merged [#721](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/721)'s checked first-Start and cross-job pathname reservations are session-only; a restored checked job can re-enter native writing without durable unverified-file refusal. [#731](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/731) and [#727](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/727) propose in-process restart/priority guards only; neither can be claimed to protect restored handles until specifically proved at the integrated head. Require persistent transfer/Place/inode identity, fail-closed/quarantined recovery before libtorrent writes, and separately hash-verified attach-existing-data on final writer implementation. Re-run native restore, priority, Start/Stop, raw Add and symlink/positive-progress destructive cases with independently immutable foreign bytes plus actual Android API29/API35/physical handset. [#568](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/issues/568) remains **G0 RED, release-blocking**. Source witness details [#568 comment](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/issues/568#issuecomment-6532745690).

## 2026-10-30 storage probe descriptor-backed creation identity

Candidate for existing [#697](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/697): descriptor repair `96df84637ae0c34da30cdf00cc569a7f39d429c8`, API29 inclusion `168c505`, preview fixture `8c00633`. Independent JDK17 control against unchanged original PR source `1c3e0986` substituted the pathname between exclusive open and its first attribute lookup: the probe recorded the foreign inode as owned, then deleted it after readback failed. Production now uses Android `Os.open(O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW|O_RDWR)`, captures identity with `fstat` on that original descriptor and retains it through cleanup. `lstat` comparisons never follow a substituted link; equal contents do not confer ownership.

**Local verification:** 13 focused JVM tests passed and test APK assembled at `96df846`. Integrated full JVM run executed **969 tests, 1 failure, 29 skips**: the sole failure was Create review UI relying on Robolectric's non-descriptor-aware `ShadowLinux.fstat(fd)`. Bytecode inspection showed it calls `stat(null)`; no production fallback or relaxed identity check was added. A read-only preview dependency and pure wording function let the UI fixture explicitly supply its synthetic destination; actual copying still independently resolves/validates the Place. At `8c00633`, all **17 selected probe/preview tests passed, zero failures/errors/skips**, and both debug APKs assembled. The previous full-run remainder passed; this is not represented as a later full-suite green. API29 fake-ADB negative controls pass with the integrated **14** storage cases.

Five Android instrumentation cases exercise the production descriptor implementation (normal cleanup, planted destination, pre-fstat source replacement, byte-identical replacement and symlink). API29 now includes that class alongside nine public Downloads/picker cases; API35 already selects it. **Actual emulator execution and final independent review remain pending.** The native rename/delete still use pathname check/action sequences; atomic external-race protection, native payload ownership and physical/provider acceptance are not proved. #568/AUD-04 remain open.

## rTorrent lifecycle outcome and capability consistency

Merged [#623](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/623) at `1c6594df1a87d17add4a00a8e2cec1f114646d5a` integrates the independently reviewed #615/#623/#644 changes while retaining their commits. Stop requires both stopped state and an inactive engine; missing activity readback stays pending. Faults or malformed replies after dispatch remain uncertain without retry. Lifecycle flags accept only integer `0`/`1`, preventing fractional or non-finite values from falsely verifying Start/Stop. Capability prechecks require the getters used by the actual operations.

The original combined source `93dfb86e5fbbb9869cbc0d53a640c97ce9bdd6af` passed full local JVM/Robolectric/native-loopback tests and debug assembly: **817 tests, 788 passed, 29 skipped, zero failures/errors**. Final source `246439df4f812945b7a1615c1372b7a1d9a15369` passed all selected RPC and capability suites plus assembly: **205 tests, 182 passed, 23 skipped, zero failures/errors**. The six new fixture methods exercise 64 real loopback gateway scenarios; red controls demonstrate escaping parser exceptions and a NaN reply falsely verifying Stop before the fixes. Cancellation still propagates and invalid hashes are refused before dispatch. Final PR head `926a0e6b91435ed179dce07b8149dab23c06bbe3` passed all six applicable checks before merge. Superseded #615 and #644 were closed as integrated through #623; no live lifecycle writes or physical-device acceptance are claimed.

Original SHA-256 `cc0e692ae196745f10abefef20afa2344d22226532465af49c20c38559c16442`; expected torrent and final SHA-256 `bbf2369f3db6526b02c08a33f18d7f6d38ad705d787db5ceebdc14dffe0e36ac`; `foreign_file_replaced=true`. The green experiment proves a desktop-native hazard, not protection.

Never mistake a matching filename or size for ownership. Android reinstall, permission revocation, SD/providers and external path races remain unverified. ## Transfer observation evidence boundaries (JVM tier)

Merged [#651](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/651), final checked source `5edd07b9c78bcee83c5d58611195c17a99f124e6`, contains three independently source-reviewed P0 safeguards, initially locally validated at [`b249982`](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/commit/b249982):

- Server inventories expire after the existing 120-second saved-list limit. Last-known rows remain visible but cannot contribute live totals, authorize a command or prove an already-satisfied batch result. Checks cover confirmation, slow capability probes and expiry between batch items, including clock rollback and invalid timestamps.
- XML-RPC decoding rejects ambiguous envelopes, missing/duplicate array data, hidden entries, nested scalar markup and duplicate struct keys. Malformed faults cannot masquerade as authoritative missing-torrent/method faults. A loopback adapter regression requires whole-inventory failure after one request without fallback.
- Native cache-flush completion/cancellation belongs to the original torrent handle. Registration and final readback share the removal monitor, while waiting releases it. Deterministic tests reject late alerts and replacement handles with the same hash; existing native loopback tests still exercise real cache-flush alerts and independent byte readback.

**Initial local validation at `b249982`:** JDK 17.0.21, Gradle 9.3.1, Android SDK 37; `gradle --no-daemon --max-workers=4 -I /workspace/.toolchains/chunks-test-proxy.gradle :app:testDebugUnitTest :app:assembleDebug` passed: **800 tests, 771 passed, 29 skipped, zero failures/errors**, with debug APK assembly. The local init script forwards the configured HTTP/HTTPS proxy to test JVMs and selects Maven Central for Robolectric; TLS and CA verification remain enabled. The first test attempt lacked those test-JVM proxy settings and was stopped after dependency-fetch failures; the corrected full run completed.

**Final local app-source validation after integrating #640:** [`3e306f7`](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/commit/3e306f7) passed the same JVM/Robolectric/native-loopback suite and debug assembly: **809 tests, 780 passed, 29 skipped, zero failures/errors**. All nine added collision-survey/native tests passed.

**Final exact-head CI:** all seven checks passed at `5edd07b9c78bcee83c5d58611195c17a99f124e6`: [Android/JVM build](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40982887639), [API35 native Add instrumentation](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40982887660), [R8 smoke](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40982887556), [lifecycle repro](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40982887637), [legacy and modern disposable rTorrent](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40982887787), and [documentation](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40982887552). The [first native run](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40981186945) failed; the revised test head aligned the unverified-result notice assertion and selected Compose's supported queued v2 scheduler before this successful run.

The 29 local opt-in/integration skips are **not** acceptance. Local JVM, synthetic RPC, desktop libtorrent and Robolectric results, plus the separately named emulator/disposable-server CI checks, do not establish physical Android, live Seedhost, real Wi-Fi/cellular handover or arbitrary storage-provider acceptance. These changes do not settle magnet/post-Add payload collisions; the failed safety-facing Start result on draft [#648](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/648#issuecomment-6511946698) remains open. [#640](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/640) is separately merged metadata-known Add preflight, not a race-free filesystem guarantee.

## 2026-10-29 per-environment capability snapshots (0A, #567)

- **Source slice:** `EnvironmentCapabilitySnapshot` grades each environment separately.
  - The phone engine's operations are `BUILT_IN`; "Copy files to this phone" is `NOT_APPLICABLE` there.
  - Each signed-in server is graded from **its own** `system.listMethods` probe as `ADVERTISED`, `MISSING` or `UNKNOWN`. A server probe cannot describe the phone.
  - It uses the separated route table carried over from #621: magnet Add and .torrent Add are graded independently, the server default Add folder is optional (its absence never refuses an explicit-destination Add) and session Remove requires `d.multicall2` + `d.hash` + `d.erase`, not `d.erase` alone.
  - `decide()` refuses when an environment is not LIVE or when an operation is MISSING or NOT_APPLICABLE. UNKNOWN is allowed, with a "not pre-checked" note, and the command's own readback decides.
  - Combined Start/Stop now runs a fresh read-only probe before taking the server's mutation lease. A server that does not advertise the Start/Stop methods refuses without being leased or sent a command. A failed probe or one without a method list stays UNKNOWN.
- **Evidence:** JVM `EnvironmentCapabilitiesTest` and `CombinedBatchLifecycleTest` (missing method refuses before lease; no method list and probe failure still run the guarded command). No real-server or device evidence yet. Advertised is not authorized.

## 2026-10-29 read-only phone file-on-disk check (TRANSFER-FILE-RECOVERY stage 1)

- **Source slice:** `LocalFileDiskCheck` and its UI, "Check files on disk (read-only)", in a phone transfer's Files panel.
  - It runs only when tapped, on the IO dispatcher, and stats each torrent file under the transfer's own save folder.
  - Each file is reported as present at the expected size, not started, skipped, partly on disk, **missing** (engine has bytes, no file), **size mismatch**, **not a file**, **unreadable** or **unsafe path**.
  - A path that escapes the save folder (`..` or a symlink) is refused, not followed.
  - It never hashes, creates, renames or deletes anything, and the summary says it checks presence and size only, not contents.
  - Non-file-path folders (for example a SAF tree URI) are explained instead of checked.
  - A missing save folder, or one that is a regular file, fails closed: every file reads **save folder unavailable**, never a clean report. A result is cleared when the save folder or file layout changes.
- **Evidence:** JVM `LocalFileDiskCheckTest` (every status, read-only assertion, symlink escape) and Robolectric `LocalFilesOnDiskCheckTest` (runs only on tap, lists the missing file, creates nothing). No device, no remote and no hash verification. Search, match, rebind and recheck stages remain open.

## 2026-10-29 opt-in device reboot repro (#569)

- **Source slice:** `LocalEngineRebootInstrumentedTest` + `tools/reboot_repro.sh` are added to the path-scoped opt-in lifecycle job.
  - **Phase A** seeds under the foreground service.
  - **`adb reboot`**, then the script waits for boot to complete. It then requires that no Chunks process is running by itself; Chunks has no boot receiver, and Android forbids starting `dataSync` from boot.
  - **Phase B** requires no engine and no ongoing notification, then a **stopped** restore with unchanged SHA-256.
- **Evidence:** this PR's `api35-fgs-timeout` job. Emulator only.

## 2026-10-29 opt-in network loss and return with real peers (#569)

- **Source slice:** `netflap=true` on the manual `android-public-swarm.yml` runs `tools/android_public_swarm_netflap.sh` (`AndroidPublicSwarmNetworkFlapInstrumentedTest`).
  - During a foreground-service download, Wi-Fi and mobile data go off for 30 s and then back on, with the test signalled at each edge.
  - **Required:** the job is not silently stopped, verified growth of ≥2 MiB resumes on the same engine, and the service survives.
  - **Recorded:** bytes during the outage and time to recovery. Connectivity is always restored on exit.
- **Merged:** [#605](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/605), squash `a2b1e23b063f495b184887754a677732b0ea6aba`.
- **Observed** ([run 40957785862](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40957785862), API35 emulator, from main at `a2b1e23`):
  - verified bytes stood at 8,650,752 at loss and 8,912,896 at return;
  - the job was **still started** (`stopped=false`) with no error;
  - verified growth resumed **20 s** after the radios returned and reached 12,845,056 bytes, on the same engine, with the service still running.
  - Emulator networking only. This does not cover a real Wi-Fi↔cellular handover, a VPN or captive portals.

## 2026-10-29 opt-in Doze observation with real peers (#569)

- **Source slice:** the manual `android-public-swarm.yml` gains `doze=true`, which runs `tools/android_public_swarm_doze.sh` (`AndroidPublicSwarmDozeInstrumentedTest`).
  - A Debian netinst download runs under the foreground service.
  - Once ≥4 MiB of verified bytes have arrived, the script unplugs the virtual battery, runs `dumpsys deviceidle force-idle`, confirms deep `IDLE` and signals the test.
  - Over 90 s the test records `GROWTH` or `STALLED` in verified bytes while idle. It requires only that the service and engine stay alive and answering.
  - The idle and battery state is restored on exit, and whether the app was on the system allowlist is logged.
- **Merged:** [#605](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/605).
- **Observed** ([run 40957780248](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40957780248), API35 emulator, from main at `a2b1e23`):
  - Chunks was **not** on the deep-idle allowlist, and deep idle was confirmed (`deep_state=IDLE`, `isDeviceIdleMode=true`).
  - The foreground-service download kept going: **GROWTH 186,384,384 verified bytes in 90 s** (6,815,744 → 193,200,128) from 192 peers.
  - This is consistent with foreground-service processes being exempt from Doze network restrictions.
  - It used forced idle on an emulator; OEM power managers, natural idle entry and the 6-hour `dataSync` budget can differ on a handset.

- **Open P0 risk (#569):** `LocalEngineService.onTimeout` still stops phone transfers via sequential per-torrent 2s readback waits plus persistence, potentially exceeding OS foreground shutdown grace on a large or blocked queue. This is a **source-review hypothesis**, not a demonstrated defect or verified repair; bounded real-engine crowded-load/timeout proof and explicit safe fail-closed semantics remain outstanding.


## 2026-10-29 independent review fixes for #577–#584

A code review of everything merged this session (`a8cb13f..2103fc7`) found these defects, now fixed:
- **Stale phone rows:** when the phone engine stopped while All environments was open, its last rows kept showing as Live and complete. They now drop to "Engine not running" with no rows.
- **Progress overflow:** server Progress sorting overflowed `Long` above about 9.2 TB completed; it now divides in floating point.
- **False "may have applied":** a server row with a non-v1 identity was refused by a local validation exception, which read as UNCERTAIN and halted the whole batch. It is now refused before any RPC, and the batch continues.
- **Saved view decoding:** a saved combined-view subset with one unreadable ID narrowed the scope instead of restoring the default.
- **Per-frame recomputation:** combined privacy decisions and sources were rebuilt on every recomposition, because profile and server objects carry fresh lambdas. They are now keyed on data.
- **Duplicated parsing:** environment-ID parsing from an alias scope existed in four copies; it is now one `EnvironmentSnapshots.serverIdOfAliasScope`. A dead `compareKnown` wrapper was also removed.

**Merged:** [#585](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/585), squash `4b9b839fceeab1c4c040b1096385085547d3dd37`, exact head `f64ca5af5294e370c7a106eed570bf37dd38b095`: [Android/JVM 40937046942](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40937046942), [R8 40937046588](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40937046588), [API35 native instrumentation 40937046886](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40937046886), [atlas 40937046865](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40937046865). **Evidence:** new JVM tests for scope parsing, overflow ordering, malformed subsets and the non-v1 refusal; full local suite green. Not changed: badge colours are read once per composition, so a colour change shows on the next screen entry.

## 2026-10-29 opt-in interrupted real-swarm download (#568, #5)

- **Source slice:** the manual `android-public-swarm.yml` gains `interrupt=true`, which runs `tools/android_public_swarm_interrupt.sh` (`AndroidPublicSwarmInterruptInstrumentedTest`).
  - **Phase A** uses the app's own engine and state directory to download part of the sanitized Debian netinst from ordinary peers, then SIGKILLs itself mid-transfer, with no Stop and no explicit save.
  - **Phase B**, in a fresh process, requires: restored stopped, no restore problems, metadata kept, and continued hash-verified growth past the pre-kill amount.
  - It **records** whether the recheck recovered the pre-kill pieces from disk (`RECOVERED`) or had to fetch them again (`REDOWNLOAD`).
- **Merged:** [#584](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/584), squash `2103fc71a4babf7953bd752c5f958cc9eff3a718`.
- **Observed** ([run 40934164720](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40934164720), API35 emulator behind the runner's NAT, from main at `2103fc7`, budget 16 MiB):
  - **Phase A:** 20,971,520 hash-verified bytes of Debian netinst `7acf8fb5…` (792,723,456 wanted) came from ordinary peers, then the process ended `Process crashed` by its own SIGKILL.
  - **Phase B** (fresh process):
    - restored **stopped** with no restore problems;
    - on Start, the recheck **RECOVERED 21,495,808 verified bytes from disk**, a little more than the last pre-kill observation, because further pieces were written in between;
    - it then continued to 25,427,968 verified bytes.
  - So resume data saved only at Add was enough for libtorrent to recheck the on-disk pieces after sudden death, with nothing lost or re-fetched.
  - One emulator run; not a physical handset, a reboot, or storage revocation.

## 2026-10-29 sudden process-death restore repro (#569)

- **Source slice:** `LocalEngineProcessDeathInstrumentedTest`, run in two processes by `tools/process_death_repro.sh` inside the opt-in lifecycle job.
  - **Phase A** seeds under the foreground service, then SIGKILLs itself. There is no Stop all, no onDestroy and no explicit save.
  - **Phase B** must find: no leftover ongoing notification, the transfer restored **stopped**, unchanged payload SHA-256, and a 100% recheck when started.
- **Merged:** [#581](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/581), squash `48c111ce0118371f5ed5888aff9145aec5f940ef`, exact head `9cd041dfd297651fcd2be1186607fa8bb3a4078d`: [Android/JVM 40919754762](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40919754762), [R8 40919754788](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40919754788), [API35 native instrumentation 40919754793](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40919754793), [native atlas 40919754797](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40919754797).
- **Observed on a disposable API35 emulator** ([run 40919754887](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40919754887)):
  - **Phase A** (pid 3991) armed transfer `1adc4f07…` and ended with `Process crashed`, its own SIGKILL.
  - **Phase B** (new pid 4100) passed: no leftover ongoing notification, restored stopped, recheck reached 1,000,000 ppm, and payload SHA-256 was unchanged.
  - Emulator only; a seeding (complete) payload, not a mid-download interruption.

## 2026-10-28 Android 10/11 foreground-service crash (found by lint, reproduced on API 29)

## 2026-10-28 Seedhost → phone payload Copy VERIFIED on the real host (#460)

- **Cause of the refusals:** rTorrent on this host is bound to `5.79.112.219` (owner: Client Area "Check IP", the same for all three instances). It reports no `network.local_address`, so Chunks fell back to the ruTorrent hostname, `5.79.112.210`, which refuses the peer port.
- **#460 (MERGED `eb79a985a547`):** exact head `b6771a5` passed Android/JVM [40755712179](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40755712179) and disposable rTorrent 0.9.8 + Arch [40755712170](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40755712170). Copy now prefers a specific `network.bind_address` over the web host, and the probe takes an optional `peer_ip`.
- **Run without an override** ([40756272768](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40756272768), main `eb79a98`, 120 s): the app found `bound=5.79.112.219` by itself. TCP was `open`, and phone and server each saw one peer. No metadata arrived within 120 s; that run's time limit was too short to show more.
- **VERIFIED** ([40756284173](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40756284173), main `eb79a98`, 900 s, `peer_ip=5.79.112.219`, the same address the app found): `CHUNKS_SEEDHOST_COPY: VERIFIED name="debian-13.7.0-amd64-netinst.iso" hash=7acf8fb5…33b0 peer=5.79.112.219:53281 bytes=792723456 metadata=true verified=792723456 maxPeers=1 seconds=782`.
  - The JVM build of the phone engine got the metadata and all 792,723,456 piece-verified bytes, with the development Seedhost as its **only** peer (no tracker, no DHT).
  - Read-only toward the server; Copy, not Move.
- **VERIFIED with auto-discovery** ([40759247300](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40759247300), main `8977283`, no override, 1200 s limit): `peer=5.79.112.219:53281 … verified=792723456 maxPeers=1 seconds=748 announced=null bound=5.79.112.219 override=none`. The app's own address choice (`network.bind_address`) copied the full ISO.
- **Not proven:** a physical handset, or the in-app button flow on the real host. Throughput was about 1 MB/s from a single peer.

## 2026-10-28 Seedhost server-to-phone copy probe with an owner-added public torrent

- The owner added the official Debian 13.7.0 netinst (public, `7acf8fb590b2…33b0`) to the primary Seedhost ruTorrent; it completed and is seeding.
- Run [40744688203](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40744688203) (main `779b725`, `max_mib=800`): **INCONCLUSIVE**. The 40 smallest complete transfers were all private, so size order never reached the Debian one.
- #455 (MERGED `5229be746763`; Android/JVM [40745292333](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40745292333), disposable rTorrent [40745292365](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40745292365)) added an optional exact `hash` input.
- Run [40746042113](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40746042113) (main `5229be7`, hash set, 900 s): the target was eligible (complete, active, public) and the phone engine was given server peer `5.79.112.210:53281` (`network.local_address` + `network.listen.port`). Result: **INCONCLUSIVE `metadata=false verified=0 maxPeers=0`**. No connection to the server's peer port was made from the GitHub runner in 900 s.
- **Meaning:** server-to-phone Copy cannot currently reach this Seedhost listener from outside. Likely causes are an inbound-blocked port or a wrong announced address/port. This is not a Chunks Copy defect; Copy against real rTorrent 0.9.8 is proven in CI (#446). #456 (MERGED `c1a2f221afb4`; Android/JVM [40748715489](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40748715489), disposable rTorrent [40748715369](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40748715369)) added that diagnosis. Run [40749519696](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40749519696) (main `c1a2f22`, 120 s) reported **`tcp=refused serverPeers=0`**. The host answers, but nothing accepts TCP on the reported port 53281, and the server has no peers on this public torrent either. The rTorrent peer listener is closed, or is not on the port `network.listen.port` reports. This is a host configuration issue for the owner (ruTorrent Settings → Connection: incoming port, "Open listening port", random port), not a Chunks defect. The owner's ruTorrent settings show "Open listening port" ticked, with the port randomized in 45000-60000. #458 (MERGED `91795194dc1a`; Android/JVM [40752978395](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40752978395), disposable rTorrent [40752978661](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40752978661)) also tests every address the RPC hostname resolves to. In run [40753615407](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40753615407) (main `9179519`), the hostname resolves only to `5.79.112.210`, the port is unchanged (`listenPortNow=53281`) and the connection is still refused. The address and port are right, but incoming peer connections are rejected before rTorrent, by a host firewall or an rTorrent bind to another interface. Next step: ask Seedhost support.

## 2026-10-27 non-destructive SVG composition workbench (#355)

- **Merged experimental four-layer editor:** PR [#355](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/355), exact PR head `d73c945f8d64dd5bd711fa9d3b802d09d09ea3ca`, squash merge `90cc3735721312991d1458afe76c8072afd4634a`. The existing paginated 815-vector gallery can now mix up to four independently identified SVG variants with drag/keyboard-accessible x/y, scale, rotation, opacity and order controls, while keeping every source file untouched. Export yields a single self-contained geometry/gradient-bearing SVG with namespaced internal IDs and an inert embedded editable recipe; recipe JSON and exported SVG can both restore an edit session. No runtime Android resource, canonical icon, font, PNG reference or transfer code changed.
- **Exact-head evidence:** [browser DOM and XML/recipe/unsafe-source tests 40618146888](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40618146888), [SVG full manifest 40618146818](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40618146818), [source reproducer 40618146886](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40618146886), [native-size glyph atlas 40618146893](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40618146893) and [all 815 vectors at 32/160 px 40618146835](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40618146835) passed. Tests include loading an exported SVG's embedded metadata into the browser-like edit interface and retaining its transforms.
- **Remaining acceptance:** actual Chrome/Android phone interaction, TalkBack/keyboard accessibility and performance at 320×700 and 412×892, background/gradient optical fidelity in exported compositions, and approved integration into native Compose all remain open. Browser SVG export must be served from same-origin HTTP(S); a downloaded `file://` page may display images but browser security can block reading their SVG source. The mixer is a *non-shipping* creative workbench, not a user-approved visual treatment.

The owner reported that remote and on-phone Adds produced visible torrent entries but **did not transfer bytes even after manually pressing Start**; and requested encrypted remembered rTorrent credentials to be opt-out. These are measured code/test changes, **not** proof the reporter's physical device now downloads.

- **Default-ON credential remembering #232:** merged `9e39c7d46c44`, exact-head `d45af2d7421ef4921f2828684ae961d972519725` passed Android/JVM [40461325109](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461325109), API35 Android Keystore instrumented [40461325093](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461325093), emulator atlas [40461325088](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461325088). AES-GCM device-bound saved rTorrent login pre-fills manual Connect on restart; opt-out and Forget erase ciphertext, no backup. Initial API35 [40460712569](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40460712569) revealed undeleted ciphertext when the key was inaccessible, fixed before acceptance. Single remembered profile only; loss/restore scenarios and optional BiometricPrompt privacy lock remain open.
- **User-approved Add-then-Start #231:** merged `0c8ae0f88a3a`; exact-head `7e2561e946c109c1defee2c096464be744903227` passed Android/JVM [40461429758](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461429758) and API35 native [40461429620](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461429620). Start-after-verified-Add selected by default, opt-out stopped-only; duplicate/pending/uncertain remote Add never starts or retries; local magnet auto-resumes only after metadata with user consent. Includes real libtorrent loopback metadata→exact-byte tests. This resolves the *UI's former stopped-only default*, but owner explicitly reported manual Start also failed, so the remote/liveness changes below are essential.
- **rTorrent full Start sequence #234:** merged `049530a80d26`; exact-head `8aa45c76066f9ea97aa8c806d4bfc57de7f85a59` passed Android/JVM [40461883682](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461883682), disposable legacy 0.9.8 + modern 0.16.x rTorrent [40461884036](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461884036), emulator atlas [40461883713](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40461883713). Remote now performs exact-hash `d.open → d.start → d.resume` and `d.stop → d.close` with no blind retry; mere `d.state=1` is no longer accepted as active because paused torrents can report it. Read-only `d.is_active`, `d.is_open`, peers and download-rate diagnostic distinguishes no active engine, zero peers and no observed byte traffic. The label in transfer cards no longer falsely says Active solely from `d.state`.
- **Public-network local engine byte proof #235:** merged `2b963737205b`; exact-head `2f2f25dcfba391f3b50a93d32e0d14d39f44022f` passed Android/JVM [40462447034](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40462447034) and real Wikimedia Commons HTTPS web-seed [40462447031](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40462447031): complete wanted bytes and exact SHA-256 after Start on Linux libtorrent4j. First attempt [40462163125](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40462163125) failed due to an incorrect assumption that `allTimeDownload` includes HTTP web-seed traffic even at 100% completion; corrected acceptance measures `totalWantedDone` and independent bytes. This **does not** establish public BitTorrent peer connections or Android handset download.
- **Local completed-byte UI #236:** merged `114b4b4a4d5b`; exact-head `5ed525b53d773d881169e59d7fe72c8fca194be5` passed Android/JVM [40462640640](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40462640640) and API35 native [40462640645](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40462640645). On-phone card now leads with independently piece-verified `doneBytes/wantedBytes`, not a transport-specific lifetime download counter; 0 peers and active upload-only mode now have explicit diagnostics.
- **Protected development Seedhost active-Start #237:** one-time trusted-main synthetic Add/Start/Stop/Erase workflow [40463480840](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40463480840) **FAILED** at `SeedhostLiveAcceptanceTest.ownerAuthorizedExactTargetMutationRoundTrip` with IllegalArgumentException before an activity result was established. The failure log did not contain sufficient sanitized phase detail to attribute the cause or prove the exact synthetic fixture was cleaned up. **Do not claim hosted Start or exact recovery and do not automatically rerun writes.** Subsequent host attempts must start with read-only reconciliation of the unique fixture and protected secret-safe diagnostics. Earlier invalid YAML [40462815550](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40462815550) never started a test job.

- **Protected read-only reconciliation #238:** merged as `b2b44441f984`; exact head `523da6d0d7465d86d3f31a7b560c1dcf2fbac031` passed Android/JVM [40464121791](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40464121791). Live protected read-only [40464540703](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40464540703) passed: precise derived fixture hash `089c6dcd18aab0a1d7b09f373bcc35d14bac8908` (`chunks-acceptance-40463480840.txt`) had **zero main-view matches and no direct d.name lookup**, with no mutations. Therefore the earlier failed synthetic fixture is absent by both checked lookups. This does **not** explain the earlier IAE or prove hosted active Start; the next step is a guarded phase-classified preflight and qualified destination selection before one further optional test mutation.

**Release gate still open:** physical Pixel Android Seedhost connect/Add/Start/peer traffic, physical Internet magnet/trackers/DHT, dataSync foreground lifetime, screen-off/reboot and actual writable SAF. No public APK should be labelled field-accepted on these results.

[#172](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/172) merged `c33b81b0761a8c88faaa16b85adb0d9bd4d241d8`, exact tested head `89689a13ce74c025f7767dc4b7b74f6b99b8aa5d` passed Android/JVM [40413678079](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40413678079) and native API35 [40413678279](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40413678279). The on-phone engine uses a distinct collection namespace. **Unproven:** physical phone, end-to-end populated protected Seedhost, TalkBack/large text, synchronization, memberships across unloaded server views and data migration.
- **Alias-aware manual Collection search:** [#173](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/173) merged `dc504a6a10daa19c893c70042777fa6f0ab43042`, exact head `bb3800cab083d4355e75047346790031c9278141` passed Android/JVM [40414746335](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40414746335) and native UI baseline [40414746890](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40414746890). Reuses one-lookup-per-transfer alias-aware search; shows original name alongside alias. No metadata modifications from searching and no proof of physical phone usability or server library-wide completeness.

- **Manual Collection view filtering:** [#175](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/175) merged `de557aa10e1341f1d4443a9343fba1454f05d43d`, exact head `882a5bd70b7f02860a2588728259c949a8a776aa` passed Android/JVM [40415918501](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40415918501) and native Android visual baseline [40415918617](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40415918617). Explicit 48dp-class selectable Collection chips filter the **currently loaded remote server view** before its 80-card cap; clear filter restores results. Pure JVM tests confirm case-insensitive exact membership; UI search still includes source names, saved aliases and paths. **Unproven:** populated Seedhost filter journey on physical phone, 200% font/TalkBack, complete all-server view, paged >80 results, persistent independent Collection registry. No automatic move/mutation.

- **Read-only native block accounting:** [#176](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/176) merged `624b2d1b8ff3b463eed1468f8f056ed14bd8624a`; exact head `039954a312d1419ec27b0fdf8740dd48fa7bd621` passed Android/JVM [40417598611](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40417598611) and API35 native suite [40417598652](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40417598652). The 50,000-entry/depth-32 no-follow lstat survey reports logical and st_blocks allocation per unique `(device,inode)`, flags possible outside hardlinks, and makes error/overflow/symlink/race/limit results incomplete. Pure JVM tests exercise hardlink dedup and overflow. The **actual Android hardlink creation fixture was skipped** because the emulator refused creating hardlinks under app-private storage; sparse/symlink/bounds native tests ran. **Unproven:** Android real-world hardlink behavior, multiple volumes/providers, concurrent writer consistency, reflinks, unique reclaim, ownership/delete permission, enforced cap and physical device. No user-facing allocated-byte readout yet.
- **Manual Collection editing pagination:** [#179](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/179) merged `fe95182d0059c3f35e34b56ac54b07f7370a0f35`, exact head `5aed30cbe94274f82d7ebe4a05240d31267f8e46` passed Android/JVM [40417953462](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40417953462) and native visual baseline [40417953469](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40417953469). Search and manual membership filter run before 40-card paging; 437-fixture JVM proof visits each result once; Previous/Next are full-width 48dp-class controls. **Not a real 437-transfer device journey**, nor physical TalkBack/200% text review or remote server full-library paging guarantee.

- **User-saved phone-folder advisory cap:** [#174](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/174) merged `2a61dafe80ea03470b3f1c340d65c06b0d6aaad2`, exact head `e3892b6dc4f3eb2f8023a55dc8bb6ea7b66dc03a` passed Android/JVM [40418037171](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40418037171) and API35 native [40418037157](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40418037157). Optional capped GiB input, persisted hashed canonical app-download root, 70/85/95/over-cap on-screen warnings/latch, fail-closed partial logical inventory, Save/Clear and non-enforcement text. Two earlier API35 failures were **test-fixture defects**: 850% instead of 85%, then one-byte floor below ceil(85%) threshold; the final fixed 90% fixture explicitly awaited the asynchronously surveyed 85%-tier warning. **Unproven:** volume-level actual allocation cap, physical handset/provider, process-death retained grant/latch behavior, write gating or deleting bytes; no automated notification or remote limit.
- **Library-copy source symlink refusal:** [#180](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/180) merged `35e348111746e7048b15d80a2f0322b18ce07a5e`, exact tested head `43a7e823d0da62a27d9556d1724dddf092057406` passed [Android/JVM 40418530525](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40418530525). Both byte-copy and source SHA-256 verification opens use NOFOLLOW; a deterministic after-preflight path→symlink swap makes publication fail and staged SAF output roll back without reading an outside file. **Still unproven:** ancestor-path swapping, inode ownership/hardlink safety, openat-equivalent lasting root confinement under arbitrary concurrent actors, device provider race and complete physical seeding integrity.

- **One-tap explicit Collection reuse:** [#184](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/184) merged `c7c9473fd511fb92414313f089f1434b14db2e8d`, exact head `968c413a16aeaa43534b8501edf585cf797b691b` passed Android/JVM [40419831414](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40419831414), native API35 [40419831400](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40419831400) and visual baseline [40419831383](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40419831383). Up to 12 previously approved Collection names are offered as explicit Add actions for remote and phone-local transfers, with case-insensitive dedup and no backing mutation. No automatic classification, physical handset/TalkBack or global Collection library claim.
- **Independent persisted Collection-name foundation:** [#186](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/186) merged `45872fdfbc97b8ce999d06ca367b3b736f3d28c8`, exact head `217ffa35b8625492205d4965fbceb71a3da073d2` passed Android/JVM [40420604929](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40420604929) and native API35 [40420604770](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40420604770). Device-local per endpoint/account or local-engine Collection names persist with no loaded transfers, validated and capped to 256 per scope. Explicit `forget` affects only reusable name, not memberships. **Native management UI is pending #188**; no server synchronization or physical user acceptance.
- **Observed allocated-block display:** [#182](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/182) merged `2a55a4b393123083ae6b27b7fa6f42d362b6e064`, exact source `612c7c331d4977c93f2d29d439f110da12e1c6c2` passed Android/JVM [40419158303](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40419158303) and API35 **attempt 2** [40419158089](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40419158089). Phone storage UI distinguishes logical and observed allocated bytes; incomplete scan is unknown, outside-hardlink warning remains visible, no reclaim/deletion guarantee. **Attempt 1 failed a preexisting native loopback transfer integrity test**: expected fixture byte but got zero at offset 180224 after FINISHED/SEEDING; unchanged source rerun passed. Open #5 records this intermittent signal; a single successful rerun does not certify native flush/payload integrity.
- **Same-volume POSIX survey fence:** [#185](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/185) merged `1da1d7e2295679ca2e2f53a1b4278917a4318277`, exact head `9131655ba95c0be4e2b4a2e8efd43ea8b669ffcd` passed Android/JVM [40420669393](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40420669393) and API35 [40420669407](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40420669407). Reject nested cross-device mount or file and impossible link-count observations, with native same-volume positive fixture and pure JVM impossible-inode coverage. **Cross-device mount injection on Android remains untested** and no ownership/unique reclaim proof is claimed.

- **Local queue native pagination:** [#187](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/187) merged `ca5816ab8edfc6488d09818a0c02f37bfb4a5ebc`; exact head `de9032877eba41f3e5f78eaf70698e2f509dcce4` passed [JVM 40421468680](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40421468680) and API35 on [40421468699 attempt 2](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40421468699). Displays 20 local torrent cards per page; deterministic 163-item corpus and native Compose page controls, **not** a real 163-torrent loaded native engine test. Initial API35 attempt failed the existing libtorrent loopback exact-byte assertion, reading a zero at offset 32768 after complete status. Do not treat the retry as closing native payload integrity #5.
- **Stale native inventory contract, model only:** [#191](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/191) merged `ecb9d6cb65c0c1e2f5de6d43f569a17786046730`; exact head `79cdd5026527b3858f742d4709b08488d0489041` passed [Android/JVM 40422821988](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40422821988). A failed native list read preserves last known transfers with stale flag and disabled-action contract; a genuinely successful empty read clears list. **LocalEngineScreen is not yet wired to this model**, and no native injected failure journey is accepted.
- **Cross-instance Collection name synchronization:** [#192](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/192) merged `ba473ced98f9a0528cb8ca78e78fa1b18bbbdc68`; exact head `484ad6253dfffebdc16fd0cf11332f69a0a66195` passed [Android/JVM 40423122043](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40423122043) and [native API35 40423121989](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40423121989). Shared in-process lock serializes registry read-modify-write across separate store objects; emulator fixture creates 18 names concurrently. **Multi-process, remote synchronization and physical-device persistence remain unproven.**
- **Finished-copy native flush requirement:** [#193](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/193) merged `f7dee900a7cc8b0a9f3d1191822877c877d1fea9`, exact head `c46eb5a9d44c5c15b597cd41f6ee1b4f4771380a` passed [Android/JVM 40423768619](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40423768619) and [native API35 40423768601](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40423768601). Explicit per-hash native `flushCache()`/`CACHE_FLUSHED` barrier is required before independent SAF copy; failure/timeout blocks publication without pausing or deleting seeding data. A genuine completed seeded fixture exercises callback. **Not a guarantee of future file immutability, power-loss durability, direct-SAF seeding, physical device background reliability, or full source piece-hash proof.**

- **Independent empty Collection native management:** [#188](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/188) merged `3588c35516931f3d30288af97c785c389175d8a6`, exact merge-resolution head `adf0386ff36442a8f4ac326b431a5c9ec4490f7f` passed Android/JVM [40424084295](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40424084295), native API35 [40424084341](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40424084341), native visual baseline [40424084282](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40424084282). Native Create empty/Manage saved names/explicit Confirm forget works on remote and local screens without changing torrent membership or filesystem; prior conflict with #187 paging was manually reconciled before this tested head. **Not** evidence for populated physical Seedhost handset, TalkBack/200% text, automatic tags, remote sync or rename/merge migration.
- **Installed alias-journey emulator synchronization:** [#194](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/194) merged `ad59a63c6bb86db067b2e56a90ac0782a6db706c`, exact head `1bf481eeb2ff58b144bc13d340516b3e8d0ce6dd` passed JVM [40424200610](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40424200610) and native API35 [40424200806](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40424200806). Installed-app test waits for both alias-search text and actual filtered `1 shown` count; retries at most three times **only** for a detached Compose Modifier node before an alias-editor click. All RPC/mutation/assertion failures still surface. This **does not** resolve the separate intermittently zero-filled local BitTorrent readback issue #5.

## Source and status rules

The current XML-RPC adapter supports typed confirmed stopped Add, Start/Stop and session-only Erase (no payload deletion request), along with transfer rates, server views and read-only file inspection. The Add screen is a native two-stage submitting flow whose real Seedhost/physical Android behavior remains unverified. The synthetic RPC tests cover authenticated fixture listing, capability absence/presence, redirect refusal and unsafe remote HTTP rejection. Most are JVM fixtures. The dedicated #15 run separately exercised the same adapter against an actual rTorrent process; this proves only the ephemeral 0.9.8 deployment, **not** authenticated remote seedbox, JSON-RPC, filesystem writes or physical Android behavior.

The build artifacts are **debug-only** and not production-qualified. No production server data was moved, renamed, deleted, copied or modified in these Chunks runs.

Future evidence must record exact source SHA, server/Android build as appropriate, sanitized environment topology, test action, verification result and remaining uncertainty. Test fixture pass means no more than the fixture asserts.

## Development server testing authorization

The owner approved destructive test operations on the designated Seedhost rTorrent instance and approved publishing nonsecret inventory and visual fixture data. **Authorization is not test evidence**: no remote Add, stop/start, remove, data erase, filesystem move or rename is considered implemented or verified because this consent exists. The approved target is the development torrent population, not credentials, other FTP paths or unrelated servers. Dedicated exact-hash gates and observed outcomes remain required.

## First controlled remote lifecycle mutation candidate

[PR #27](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/27) proposes an allowlisted exact-v1-hash start/stop adapter with state read-back and no automatic retries. It merged at `ef03e099011f796118f0626bcc32aa1cadf045fa` following [CI run 40064096463](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40064096463), PR SHA `124cee10a2468a88eb0cb576bb308ae944baf97c`, successful JVM tests and debug APK assembly. **Actual live-server start/stop remains untested**. Pending/uncertain outcomes must never be displayed as verified success.

## Public-source fixture factory

The [Archive/Commons collector](PUBLIC-TORRENT-FIXTURES.md) stages strict validation, exact infohash identity and metadata-only provider retrieval. The collector code and offline tests passed for merged #29/#30 ([runs 40064941665](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40064941665), [40065113278](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40065113278)); real Archive/Commons download and live swarm viability have not yet been independently observed. Collector does not contact rTorrent, download full media, or prove application Add, seeding or lifecycle behavior.

## Remote Add readback prototype

The stopped Add API in [merged PR #31](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/31) passed [Android/JVM run 40065534962](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40065534962) at SHA `68c8406ca43f6a7b5a1f6504127cfe4b9fbf8eff` but still requires real-server acceptance. Synthetic RPC acknowledgements, if successful, prove command sequencing only. `VerifiedStopped` requires observed matching main-view hash, `d.directory_base` and stopped state. Pending/uncertain results must not be reported as success. No automatic retry or cleanup on uncertainty; external live test is a distinct gate.

## Public Add protocol acceptance harness

[Merged PR #32](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/pull/32) passed the **offline** suite in [run 40065967589](https://github.com/gamesbyian/Chunks/actions/runs/40065967589), SHA `2378f2701d637d64e60802d83ced80f4793485c5`. A separate manual-only [Seedhost Add proof](../.github/workflows/seedhost-public-add-smoke.yml) now tests exact-hash-confirmed `load.raw_verbose` and records readback status. Before a real manual run, this is **offline-tested scaffolding only**, not proof of actual Seedhost Add, binary XML-RPC interoperability, physical file bytes, or an Android Add flow. `already_present_no_write`, `acknowledged_pending_readback` and `outcome_uncertain` are not successful new Adds.

## Real snapshot visual QA selector

The proposed `tools/seedbox_fixture_qa.py` works on the opt-in development Seedhost main-view snapshot only. It selects representative **real** transfer cases and records missing UI states without synthesizing names. The script's offline tests, and the enhanced secret-backed snapshot pipeline, require separate evidence. It is **not** a screenshot capture, a live inventory completeness proof, or any transfer mutation.
